Title: Materials towards a History of the American Baptists, in XII volumes (vol. I: Pennsylvania)
Creator: Morgan Edwards (1722-1795), Fellow of Rhode Island College and overseer of the Baptist church in Philadelphia
Publisher: Philadelphia: printed by Joseph Crukshank and Isaac Collins
Publication Date: 1770
Rights: Public Domain (published 1770, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item materials00edwa (https://archive.org/details/materials00edwa), the item's own materials00edwa_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1770. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-16. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: ac2e2314f9d64228b5a96ea932d34b041be9b346464e764df6e4f9e48e72b612.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Library noise before the title page is left in place. Long s is read as f throughout this OCR ("diftinction", "worfhip").
Content note: A contemporary Baptist outsider's survey of every Baptist congregation in Pennsylvania in 1770, including the Tunkers (Brethren) and Ephrata with membership figures and leaders' names; an outside witness for the Brethren world and an inside witness for colonial Baptists.
